- Canonize (status='canon'—user-supplied canon per project rules) # - Export CSV + ZIP import os, re, uuid, json, zipfile, io, datetime, difflib from pathlib import Path import pandas as pd from caas_jupyter_tools import display_dataframe_to_user now_iso = datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat() src_path = Path("/mnt/data/VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt") if not src_path.exists(): raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source canon file not found at src_path ") raw = src_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") # --- Extraction & splitting --- # Keep page/line anchors by tracking original line numbers lines = raw.splitlines() # Simple sentence splitter that respects ., !, ?, em-dash breaks, and blank lines as hard stops def split_into_sentences(text): # Replace unusual whitespace text = re.sub(r' +', ' ', text).strip() # Pre-split on sentence enders parts = re.split(r'(?<=[\.\?\!]) +(?=[A-Z0-9"', text) # Further split on long dashes if they create multiple claims out = [] for p in parts: for sub in re.split(r' +— +| +- +', p): s = sub.strip() if s: out.append(s) return out candidates = [] provenance = [] for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1): # If line is blank, skip if not line.strip(): continue # If the line is already short, consider as a single candidate, else sentence-split if len(line.strip()) <= 320: parts = split_into_sentences(line.strip()) else: parts = split_into_sentences(line.strip()) for s in parts: s_clean = s.strip() if s_clean: candidates.append(s_clean) provenance.append(f"VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt:L i ") # --- Normalizer (basic) --- def normalize_text(t): t = re.sub(r' +', ' ', t).strip() # standardize quotes t = t.replace("“","\"").replace("”","\"").replace("’","'").replace("‘","'") return t normed = [normalize_text(s) for s in candidates] # --- Enforce atom size (<=280 chars), prefer to cut on clause boundaries if needed --- def trim_atomic(t, max_len=280): if len(t) <= max_len: return t # Try to cut at the last punctuation before the limit cut = t[:max_len] m = re.search(r'^[ ] 0,%d (?<=[:;\.\?\!])' % (max_len,), t) if m: trimmed = m.group(0).strip() if len(trimmed) >= int(max_len*0.5): return trimmed # Else hard cut return cut.rstrip() atoms = [trim_atomic(t) for t in normed] # --- Deduper (exact + fuzzy) --- unique = [] unique_src = [] seen = set() for idx, a in enumerate(atoms): key = a.lower() if key in seen: continue # fuzzy compare with last N (window) to keep it O(n * window) window = 200 dup_found = False for b in unique[-window:]: ratio = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, a.lower(), b.lower()).ratio() if ratio >= 0.92: # Jaccard>=0.92 proxy using seq ratio dup_found = True break if not dup_found: seen.add(key) unique.append(a) unique_src.append(provenance[idx]) # --- Build shards dataframe --- def make_id(): return "shard_" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12] df = pd.DataFrame( "id": [make_id() for _ in unique], "verbatim": unique, "created_at": [now_iso]*len(unique), "origin_pointer": unique_src, "status": ["canon"]*len(unique), # user-provided canon "provenance": [["user:viscera_canon"]]*len(unique), "collection": ["" for _ in unique], "entity_ids": [[] for _ in unique], ) # Save CSVs to /mnt/data out_dir = Path("/mnt/data/shattercore_output") out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) shards_csv = out_dir/"shards.csv" df.to_csv(shards_csv, index=False) # I pushed the full VISCERA canon through a lite Shattercore pass (extract → split → normalize → dedupe → canonize) and exported files. - [Download shards.csv](sandbox:/mnt/data/shattercore_output/shards.csv) - [Download the full bundle (ZIP)](sandbox:/mnt/data/shattercore_output.zip) I also popped open a preview of the first 200 shards so you can spot-check.

Construct a meticulous detailed timeline. **Assistant (2025-10-15 08:10:07 UTC):** # Comprehensive Shattercore pass over all project files: # - Sources: VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt (canon), SHATTERCORE_ALL.txt (framework/spec) # - Steps: Extract → Split → Normalize → Trim (<=280) → Dedupe (exact + punctuation-stripped) # - Dating: regex date capture + multi-format parser → ISO-8601 # - Events: build from shards with dates; infer titles; attach provenance # - Timeline: chronological CSV # - Logistics: basic chronology checks (overlaps, tight clusters, rest days) without external geocoding # - Exports: shards.csv, events.csv, timeline.csv, chronology_issues.csv, logistics_report.csv, manifest.json, ZIP import re, uuid, json, zipfile, datetime from pathlib import Path import pandas as pd from caas_jupyter_tools import display_dataframe_to_user # ---------- Helpers ---------- def now_iso(): return datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat() def read_text_if_exists(p: Path): return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") if p.exists() else "" def normalize_text(t: str) -> str: t = re.sub(r' +', ' ', t).strip() t = t.replace("“","\"").replace("”","\"").replace("’","'").replace("‘","'") return t def split_into_sentences(text: str): text = re.sub(r' +', ' ', text).strip() if not text: return [] # Split on sentence enders followed by capital/quote/number parts = re.split(r'(?<=[\.\?\!]) +(?=[A-Z0-9"', text) out = [] for p in parts: # Further split on em/en dashes or hyphens used as clause breaks for sub in re.split(r' +— +| +- +', p): s = sub.strip() if s: out.append(s) return out def trim_atomic(t: str, max_len=280): if len(t) <= max_len: return t # Prefer to cut at punctuation before limit m = re.search(r'^[ ] 0,%d (?<=[:;\.\?\!])' % max_len, t) if m and len(m.group(0).strip()) >= int(max_len*0.5): return m.group(0).strip() return t[:max_len].rstrip() def make_id(prefix="shard"): return f" prefix _" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12] # Date parser (multi-format attempts) DATE_PATTERNS = [ r' 4 - 2 - 2 ', # 2025-10-15 r' 1,2 / 1,2 / 2,4 ', # 10/15/2025 or 10/15/25 r' (?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]* + 1,2 (?:, * 4 )? ', # Oct 15, 2025 / October 15 r' 1,2 +(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]* + 4 ', # 15 Oct 2025 ] MONTHS = m:i for i,m in enumerate(["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dec"], start=1) MONTHS_FULL = "january":1,"february":2,"march":3,"april":4,"may":5,"june":6, "july":7,"august":8,"september":9,"sept":9,"october":10,"november":11,"december":12 def try_parse_date(s: str): s = s.strip() # ISO try: return datetime.date.fromisoformat(s) except Exception: pass # mm/dd/yyyy or mm/dd/yy if re.fullmatch(r' 1,2 / 1,2 / 2,4 ', s): mm,dd,yy = s.split("/") mm,dd = int(mm), int(dd) yy = int(yy) if yy < 100: # assume 20xx for 2-digit yy += 2000 if yy <= 69 else 1900 return datetime.date(yy, mm, dd) # "Oct 15, 2025" / "October 15, 2025" / "Oct 15" m = re.match(r'([A-Za-z]+) +( 1,2 )(?:, *( 4 ))?$', s) if m: month_name, day, year = m.group(1), int(m.group(2)), m.group(3) month = MONTHS.get(month_name[:3].title()) if month: if year: return datetime.date(int(year), month, day) # If year missing, leave as None (cannot infer reliably here) return None # "15 Oct 2025" m = re.match(r'( 1,2 ) +([A-Za-z]+) +( 4 )$', s) if m: day, month_name, year = int(m.group(1)), m.group(2).lower(), int(m.group(3)) month = MONTHS_FULL.get(month_name[:3], MONTHS_FULL.get(month_name)) if month: return datetime.date(year, month, day) return None def extract_first_date(text: str): for pat in DATE_PATTERNS: m = re.search(pat, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) if m: raw = m.group(0) try_date = try_parse_date(raw) return raw, try_date return None, None # Simple location heuristic LOC_PATTERNS = [ r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+, *[A-Z] 2 ', # City, ST r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+, *[A-Za-z ]+(?:USA|United States|UK|Canada) ', r' +([A-Z][ .\- ]+?), *([A-Z][a-zA-Z]+) ', # at Venue, City ] def extract_location(text: str): for pat in LOC_PATTERNS: m = re.search(pat, text) if m: if len(m.groups()) >= 2: return f" m.group(1) , m.group(2) " return m.group(0) return "" # ---------- Load sources ---------- src_files = [ Path("/mnt/data/VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt"), Path("/mnt/data/SHATTERCORE_ALL.txt"), ] sources = [] for p in src_files: if p.exists(): sources.append((p.name, read_text_if_exists(p).splitlines())) if not sources: raise FileNotFoundError("No project files were found.") # ---------- Build candidate shards ---------- rows = [] for fname, lines in sources: for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1): if not line.strip(): continue for sent in split_into_sentences(line): verb = normalize_text(sent) if not verb: continue verb = trim_atomic(verb, 280) rows.append( "verbatim": verb, "origin_pointer": f" fname :L i ", "source_file": fname, ) df = pd.DataFrame(rows) if df.empty: raise RuntimeError("No shard candidates were extracted.") # ---------- Deduplicate (exact + punctuation-stripped) ---------- punct_re = re.compile(r'[ _]+', re.UNICODE) df["key_exact"] = df["verbatim"].str.lower() df["key_loose"] = df["key_exact"].apply(lambda s: punct_re.sub('', s)) df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["key_exact"]) df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["key_loose"]) # ---------- Dating & locations ---------- dates_raw = [] dates_iso = [] for t in df["verbatim"].tolist(): raw, d = extract_first_date(t) dates_raw.append(raw if raw else "") dates_iso.append(d.isoformat() if d else "") df["date_text"] = dates_raw df["date"] = dates_iso df["location_guess"] = df["verbatim"].apply(extract_location) # ---------- Finalize shards ---------- df["id"] = [make_id("shard") for _ in range(len(df))] df["created_at"] = now_iso() # Canonization status: user-provided canon for VISCERA, spec lines remain unverified df["status"] = df["source_file"].apply(lambda s: "canon" if "VISCERA" in s else "unverified") df["provenance"] = df["source_file"].apply(lambda s: ["user:viscera_canon"] if "VISCERA" in s else ["project:shattercore_spec"]) df["collection"] = "" df["entity_ids"] = [[] for _ in range(len(df))] # ---------- Events & Timeline ---------- # Event rows from shards with a parsed date events = df[df["date"] != ""].copy() events["event_id"] = [make_id("event") for _ in range(len(events))] events["title"] = events["verbatim"].str.slice(0, 120) events["time"] = "" events["location"] = events["location_guess"] events["links"] = "" # Timeline: chronological order timeline = events.copy() timeline["date_dt"] = pd.to_datetime(timeline["date"], errors="coerce") timeline = timeline.sort_values(["date_dt", "event_id"]) # ---------- Chronology & Logistics Checks ---------- issues = [] # 1) Overlaps/duplicates per date with near-identical lines (simple loose key) dupe_counts = timeline.groupby(["date", "key_loose"]).size().reset_index(name="count") dupe_counts = dupe_counts[dupe_counts["count"] > 1] for _, r in dupe_counts.iterrows(): issues.append( "issue_type": "duplicate_event_text_same_date", "date": r["date"], "detail": f" int(r['count']) events share similar text (key_loose= r['key_loose'][:24] ...)" ) # 2) Tight clusters: more than 7 events in any 7-day window (rest day policy) if not timeline.empty: dt_series = timeline["date_dt"].dropna().sort_values() if not dt_series.empty: dt_list = dt_series.tolist() i = 0 for i, start in enumerate(dt_list): # count events within 7 days inclusive window_end = start + pd.Timedelta(days=6) count = sum(1 for d in dt_list if start <= d <= window_end) if count > 7: issues.append( "issue_type": "rest_day_violation_cluster", "date": start.date().isoformat(), "detail": f" count events scheduled within 7 days" ) # 3) Same location repeated with identical date (possible double-booking) if not timeline.empty: dbl = timeline.groupby(["date", "location"]).size().reset_index(name="n") dbl = dbl[(dbl["location"] != "") & (dbl["n"] > 1)] for _, r in dbl.iterrows(): issues.append( "issue_type": "double_booking_location_date", "date": r["date"], "detail": f" r['n'] events at r['location'] on the same date" ) chronology_issues = pd.DataFrame(issues) # Logistics report (basic counts) logistics = [] if not timeline.empty: by_day = timeline.groupby("date").size().reset_index(name="events_count") for _, r in by_day.iterrows(): logistics.append( "date": r["date"], "events_count": int(r["events_count"]), "rest_day_flag": r["events_count"] > 1 # simplistic: multiple events same day may stress logistics ) logistics_df = pd.DataFrame(logistics) # ---------- Exports ---------- out_dir = Path("/mnt/data/shattercore_full_output") out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) # Core exports cols_shards = ["id","verbatim","created_at","origin_pointer","status","provenance","collection","entity_ids","date","date_text","location_guess","source_file"] shards_csv = out_dir/"shards.csv" df[cols_shards].to_csv(shards_csv, index=False) events_csv = out_dir/"events.csv" events[["event_id","title","date","time","location","links","origin_pointer","id"]].to_csv(events_csv, index=False) timeline_csv = out_dir/"timeline.csv" timeline[["event_id","date","location","title","origin_pointer","id"]].to_csv(timeline_csv, index=False) chronology_csv = out_dir/"chronology_issues.csv" (chronology_issues if not chronology_issues.empty else pd.DataFrame(columns=["issue_type","date","detail"])).to_csv(chronology_csv, index=False) logistics_csv = out_dir/"logistics_report.csv" (logistics_df if not logistics_df.empty else pd.DataFrame(columns=["date","events_count","rest_day_flag"])).to_csv(logistics_csv, index=False) # Empty linkage/characters/albums/songs/tour_dates for placeholders for name in ["edges","characters","albums","songs","tour_dates"]: pd.DataFrame().to_csv(out_dir/f" name .csv", index=False) manifest = "generated": now_iso(), "sources": [str(p) for p in src_files if p.exists()], "counts": "shards_total": int(len(df)), "events_with_dates": int(len(events)), "timeline_rows": int(len(timeline)), "chronology_issues": int(len(chronology_issues)), , "notes": [ "Dates are normalized to ISO-8601 when year is available; undated shards retained.", "Logistics checks are offline-safe (no geocoding): rest-day clusters, double-booking, duplicate text on same date.", "Further feasibility (drive times, venue capacities) can be added if structured tour data/venues are present." ] (out_dir/"manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8") zip_path = Path("/mnt/data/shattercore_full_output.zip") with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf: for child in out_dir.iterdir(): zf.write(child, arcname=child.name) # ---------- Previews ---------- display_dataframe_to_user("Shattercore — Shards (preview)", df.head(150)) display_dataframe_to_user("Shattercore — Timeline (preview)", timeline.head(150)) print(f"[OK] Exported: - shards_csv - events_csv - timeline_csv - chronology_csv - logistics_csv - ZIP: zip_path ") print(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2)) **Assistant (2025-10-15 08:10:53 UTC):** # df["persons"] = df["persons"].apply(safe_listify) # Fallback simple name detection when persons empty: multi-token caps sequences NAME_RE = re.compile(r' ([A-Z][a-z]+(?: +[A-Z][a-z]+) 1,2 ) ') def fallback_names(text): if not isinstance(text, str): return [] cands = NAME_RE.findall(text) # filter common false positives stop = "The","A","An","Of","And","Or","In","On","At","For","To","From","By","Is","Are","Be","With","As","But","If","Then","So","It","I","We","You","They","He","She" out = [] for n in cands: toks = n.split() if any(t in stop for t in toks): continue if len(toks) >= 2: out.append(n) # dedupe preserve order seen=set(); ret=[] for n in out: if n not in seen: seen.add(n); ret.append(n) return ret df["persons"] = df.apply(lambda r: r["persons"] if r["persons"] else fallback_names(str(r["verbatim"])), axis=1) # ---- Alias merge ---- def normalize_alias(n): n = re.sub(r'[^A-Za-z ]', '', str(n)).strip().lower() n = re.sub(r' +', ' ', n) return n # Build canonical mapping by first-last model name_counts = for plist in df["persons"]: for n in plist: key = normalize_alias(n) if not key: continue name_counts[key] = name_counts.get(key, 0) + 1 # Choose canonical as most frequent variant per key group (key already normalized) canonical_for = for key in name_counts.keys(): parts = key.split() if len(parts) >= 2: canon = " ".join([p.capitalize() for p in parts]) else: canon = key.capitalize() canonical_for[key] = canon # Apply mapping to shards df["characters"] = df["persons"].apply(lambda lst: sorted( canonical_for.get(normalize_alias(n), n) for n in lst if n )) # Build alias map table alias_rows = [] for key, canon in canonical_for.items(): alias_rows.append( "canonical_name": canon, "alias_norm": key ) alias_df = pd.DataFrame(alias_rows).drop_duplicates().sort_values(["canonical_name","alias_norm"]) alias_df.to_csv(OUTDIR/"alias_map.csv", index=False) # ---- Time normalization ---- def parse_time_of_day(s): if not isinstance(s, str): return None s=s.strip() if not s: return None # Accept HH:MM or HH:MM AM/PM m = re.match(r'^( 1,2 ):( 2 )(?: *([AaPp][Mm]))?$', s) if not m: return None hh, mm, ap = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), m.group(3) if ap: ap = ap.lower() if ap == "pm" and hh != 12: hh += 12 if ap == "am" and hh == 12: hh = 0 return f" hh:02d : mm:02d " def ensure_date(s): s = str(s).strip() if not s: return "" # expect ISO-like YYYY-MM-DD if re.match(r'^ 4 - 2 - 2 $', s): return s # mm/dd/yy m = re.match(r'^( 1,2 )/( 1,2 )/( 2,4 )$', s) if m: mm,dd,yy = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3)) if yy < 100: yy += 2000 if yy <= 69 else 1900 return f" yy:04d - mm:02d - dd:02d " return "" df["date_iso"] = df["date"].apply(ensure_date) df["time_iso"] = df["time"].apply(parse_time_of_day) # Synthetic day bucket for undated narratives # We'll create a pseudo-date order using file/line order captured in origin_pointer def parse_origin_line(op): m = re.search(r':L( +) ', str(op)) return int(m.group(1)) if m else 0 df["origin_line"] = df["origin_pointer"].apply(parse_origin_line) df["source_order"] = df["source_file"].astype(str) + ":" + df["origin_line"].astype(str) # ---- Duration estimation ---- def explicit_span_seconds(text): # phrases like "for 2 hours", "for 15 minutes", "for 30 sec" text = str(text).lower() m = re.search(r' +( 1,3 ) *(hours|hour|hrs|hr|minutes|minute|min|mins|seconds|second|sec|secs) ', text) if not m: return None n, unit = int(m.group(1)), m.group(2) if unit.startswith("hour") or unit in "hrs","hr" : return n*3600 if unit.startswith("min"): return n*60 if unit.startswith("sec"): return n return None WPS = PARAMS["reading_speed_wpm"]/60.0 # words per second MIN_S = PARAMS["min_shard_seconds"] MAX_S = PARAMS["max_shard_minutes"]*60 def shard_duration_seconds(text): span = explicit_span_seconds(text) if span: return max(MIN_S, min(MAX_S, span)) words = len(str(text).split()) est = math.ceil(words / max(1e-6, WPS)) return max(MIN_S, min(MAX_S, est)) df["duration_sec"] = outputs import re, json, ast, uuid, datetime, zipfile from pathlib import Path import pandas as pd from caas_jupyter_tools import display_dataframe_to_user # ---------- Helpers ---------- def read_lines(p: Path): return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines() if p.exists() else [] def normalize_text(t: str) -> str: t = re.sub(r' +', ' ', t).strip() return t.replace("“","\"").replace("”","\"").replace("’","'").replace("‘","'") def split_into_sentences(text: str): text = re.sub(r' +', ' ', text).strip() if not text: return [] parts = re.split(r'(?<=[\.\?\!]) +(?=[A-Z0-9"', text) out = [] for p in parts: for sub in re.split(r' +— +| +- +', p): s = sub.strip() if s: out.append(s) return out def trim_atomic(t: str, max_len=280): if len(t) <= max_len: return t m = re.search(r'^[ ] 0,%d (?<=[:;\.\?\!])' % max_len, t) if m and len(m.group(0).strip()) >= int(max_len*0.5): return m.group(0).strip() return t[:max_len].rstrip() def extract_first_date(text: str): DATE_PATTERNS = [ r' 4 - 2 - 2 ', r' 1,2 / 1,2 / 2,4 ', r' (?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]* + 1,2 (?:, * 4 )? ', r' 1,2 +(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]* + 4 ', ] for pat in DATE_PATTERNS: m = re.search(pat, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) if m: raw = m.group(0) d = try_parse_date(raw) return raw, (d.isoformat() if d else "") return "", "" MONTHS = m:i for i,m in enumerate(["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dec"], start=1) MONTHS_FULL = "january":1,"february":2,"march":3,"april":4,"may":5,"june":6,"july":7,"august":8,"september":9,"sept":9,"october":10,"november":11,"december":12 def try_parse_date(s: str): s = s.strip() try: return datetime.date.fromisoformat(s) except: pass if re.fullmatch(r' 1,2 / 1,2 / 2,4 ', s): mm,dd,yy = s.split("/"); mm,dd=int(mm),int(dd); yy=int(yy) if yy < 100: yy += 2000 if yy <= 69 else 1900 return datetime.date(yy, mm, dd) m = re.match(r'([A-Za-z]+) +( 1,2 )(?:, *( 4 ))?$', s) if m: month_name, day, year = m.group(1), int(m.group(2)), m.group(3) month = MONTHS.get(month_name[:3].title()) if month: return datetime.date(int(year), month, day) if year else None m = re.match(r'( 1,2 ) +([A-Za-z]+) +( 4 )$', s) if m: day, month_name, year = int(m.group(1)), m.group(2).lower(), int(m.group(3)) month = MONTHS_FULL.get(month_name[:3], MONTHS_FULL.get(month_name)) if month: return datetime.date(year, month, day) return None def extract_time(text: str): m = re.search(r' ([01]? |2[0-3]):[0-5] (?: *(?:AM|PM|am|pm))? ', text) return m.group(0) if m else "" def extract_location(text: str): LOC_PATTERNS = [ r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+, *[A-Z] 2 ', r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+, *(?:USA|United States|UK|Canada) ', r' +([A-Z][ .\- ]+?), *([A-Z][a-zA-Z]+) ', r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+(?: +[A-Z][a-zA-Z]+)* +(?:Hall|Theatre|Theater|Arena|Club|Bar|Center|Centre|Pavilion|Stadium) ', r' +([A-Z][A-Za-z]+(?: +[A-Z][A-Za-z]+)*) ' ] for pat in LOC_PATTERNS: m = re.search(pat, text) if m: if len(m.groups()) >= 2: return f" m.group(1) , m.group(2) " return m.group(0) return "" def extract_person_candidates(text: str): STOP = "The","A","An","Of","And","Or","In","On","At","For","To","From","By","Is","Are","Be","With","As","But","If","Then","So","It","I","We","You","They","He","She" tokens = re.findall(r' [A-Z][a-zA-Z]+\.? ', text) names = [] i=0 while i < len(tokens): seq=[tokens[i]]; j=i+1 while j < len(tokens) and re.match(r'^[A-Z][a-zA-Z]+\.?$', tokens[j]): seq.append(tokens[j]); j+=1 if 1 <= len(seq) <= 4 and not any(w in STOP for w in seq) and len(seq)>=2: names.append(" ".join(seq)) i=j seen=set(); out=[] for n in names: if n not in seen: seen.add(n); out.append(n) return out # ---------- Ingest ---------- src_files = [ Path("/mnt/data/VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt"), Path("/mnt/data/SHATTERCORE_ALL.txt"), ] rows = [] for p in src_files: lines = read_lines(p) for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1): if not line.strip(): continue for sent in split_into_sentences(line): verb = trim_atomic(normalize_text(sent), 280) if verb: rows.append( "verbatim":verb, "origin_pointer":f" p.name :L i ", "source_file":p.name ) df = pd.DataFrame(rows) # Dedupe punct_re = re.compile(r'[ _]+', re.UNICODE) df["key_exact"] = df["verbatim"].str.lower() df["key_loose"] = df["key_exact"].apply(lambda s: punct_re.sub('', s)) df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["key_exact"]).drop_duplicates(subset=["key_loose"]).reset_index(drop=True) # Enrichment df["date_text"], df["date"] = zip(*[extract_first_date(t) for t in df["verbatim"]]) df["time"] = [extract_time(t) for t in df["verbatim"]] df["location"] = [extract_location(t) for t in df["verbatim"]] df["persons"] = [extract_person_candidates(t) for t in df["verbatim"]] # Tags & subtags TOPICS = "recording": ["recorded","recording","studio","mix","master","engineer","producer","session","take","demo"], "release": ["release","out","drop","premiere","announce","preorder","vinyl","pressing","stream"], "performance": ["live","show","gig","concert","set","festival","tour","support","opening","encore","soundcheck"], "tour": ["tour","leg","date","venue","city","route","load-in","capacity"], "collaboration": ["feat.","featuring","with","collab","co-write","duet","remix"], "lyrics": ["lyrics","verse","chorus","bridge","hook","line","lyric"], "media": ["interview","press","review","magazine","article","podcast","radio","TV","zine"], "award": ["award","nominated","won","shortlist","longlist"], "theme": ["theme","motif","symbol","metaphor","philosophy"], "lore": ["lore","canon","shard","world","character","backstory"], "conflict": ["dispute","conflict","controversy","quarantine","contradiction"], "logistics": ["drive","miles","hours","capacity","sold out","sellout","load","routing","rest day"], "finance": ["budget","cost","revenue","sales","streams","certified","units"], "tech": ["synth","sampler","drum machine","DAW","plugin","patch","preset","MIDI","tape","analog","digital"] SUBTOPICS = "recording": "tracking":["track","take","mic","amp","booth"], "mixing":["mix","bus","compressor","eq","reverb"], "mastering":["master","limiter","lufs"] , "performance": "setlist":["setlist","opener","closer"], "production":["lights","backline","monitor","FOH"] , "release": "formats":["vinyl","cassette","cd","digital"], "promo":["premiere","trailer","teaser"] , "tour": "routing":["route","drive","miles","hours"], "venues":["venue","capacity","upgrade","downgrade"] , def classify(text): t = text.lower() tags=set() for topic, kws in TOPICS.items(): if any(k in t for k in kws): tags.add(topic) subs=set() for parent, groups in SUBTOPICS.items(): if parent in tags: for sub, kws in groups.items(): if any(k in t for k in kws): subs.add(f" parent : sub ") # collection if "tour" in tags or "performance" in tags: col="events" elif "release" in tags or "recording" in tags or "lyrics" in tags: col="lore_notes" elif "character" in t or "backstory" in t: col="characters" else: col="lore_notes" return col, sorted(tags), sorted(subs) cols, tags, subs = [],[],[] for v in df["verbatim"]: c,tg,sb = classify(v); cols.append(c); tags.append(tg); subs.append(sb) df["collection"]=cols; df["tags"]=tags; df["subtags"]=subs # Status/provenance + IDs df["id"] = [f"shard_ uuid.uuid4().hex[:12] " for _ in range(len(df))] df["created_at"] = datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat() df["status"] = df["source_file"].apply(lambda s: "canon" if "VISCERA" in s else "unverified") df["provenance"] = df["source_file"].apply(lambda s: ["user:viscera_canon"] if "VISCERA" in s else ["project:shattercore_spec"]) # ---------- Alias-merge characters ---------- def norm_name(n): n = re.sub(r' +', ' ', str(n)).strip() n = re.sub(r'[^ ]', '', n) return n.lower() name_freq= for lst in df["persons"]: for n in lst: name_freq[n]=name_freq.get(n,0)+1 clusters= for n in name_freq: clusters.setdefault(norm_name(n), []).append(n) def canonicalize(options): return sorted(options, key=lambda x: (-name_freq.get(x,0), -len(x.split()), x))[0].title() alias_map = o: canonicalize(opts) for k,opts in clusters.items() for o in opts df["characters"] = df["persons"].apply(lambda lst: sorted( alias_map.get(n,n) for n in lst if n )) chars_sorted = sorted( c for lst in df["characters"] for c in lst ) char_ids = c: f"char_ uuid.uuid4().hex[:10] " for c in chars_sorted characters_df = pd.DataFrame( "character_id":[char_ids[c] for c in chars_sorted],"name":chars_sorted,"aliases":[[] for _ in chars_sorted] ) # ---------- Duration & ordering ---------- def estimate_duration_seconds(text, tags): t = text.lower() words = max(1, len(re.findall(r' +', text))) base = words * 0.4 tagset = set(tags or []) if "performance" in tagset or re.search(r' |gig|set|festival|concert ', t): return int(max(1800, words*1.5)) if "tour" in tagset: return int(max(600, words*0.8)) if "recording" in tagset: if re.search(r' (ing)? ', t): return 90*60 if re.search(r' (ing)? ', t): return 120*60 if re.search(r' |take|session ', t): return 240*60 return 120*60 if "media" in tagset or re.search(r' |podcast|radio ', t): return 45*60 if "transition" in tagset or re.search(r' to ', t): return 30 if text.count('"') >= 2 or re.search(r'“.+?”', text): return int(max(30, words*0.6)) return int(max(20, base)) df["duration_sec"] = [estimate_duration_seconds(v, tg) for v,tg in zip(df["verbatim"], df["tags"])] def parse_iso_date_safe(x): try: if pd.isna(x) or not str(x): return None return pd.to_datetime(str(x)).date() except: return None df["date_parsed"] = [parse_iso_date_safe(x) for x in df["date"]] def line_no(ptr): if isinstance(ptr, str) and ":L" in ptr: try: return int(ptr.split(":L")[-1]) except: return 10**9 return 10**9 df["line_no"] = df["origin_pointer"].apply(line_no) df = df.sort_values(by=["date_parsed","source_file","line_no"], na_position="last").reset_index(drop=True) # Each lower level is a literal subset of the next. | Level | Shard Type | Description | Example Naming | |-------|-------------|--------------|----------------| | **S0** | **Atom Shard** | smallest linguistic unit — a word or symbol with meaning; base element of language frequency analysis | `shard.atom.0000001` | | **S1** | **Line Shard** | one line or sentence — minimal readable shard | `shard.line.00032` | | **S2** | **Verse / Paragraph Shard** | group of adjacent line shards in one setting or breath | `shard.verse.0012` | | **S3** | **Scene Shard** | logical unit of narrative action; spatial-temporal continuity | `shard.scene.0047` | | **S4** | **Chapter Shard** | grouping of scenes unified by focus or chronology | `shard.chapter.02A` | | **S5** | **Cycle / Group Shard** | thematic cluster (e.g. recording cycle, tour leg, dream sequence) | `shard.group.tour_leg_1` | | **S6** | **Part Shard** | macro-division (e.g. *Part I: Wound Atlas*) | `shard.part.I` | | **S7** | **Interlude Shard** | temporal or tonal transition — flashback, montage, insert | `shard.interlude.Δ1` | | **S8** | **Book Shard** | the entire consolidated canon (the totality) | `shard.book.VISCERA` | Each structural shard will have: - `level_index` - `parent_shard_id` - `child_shard_ids[]` - `title` (auto-generated or extracted) - `span_start_iso` / `span_end_iso` - `duration_sec_total` --- ### ⚖️ II.

They tell **how** the structure shards are connected or re-ordered. | Type | Purpose | Example | |------|----------|----------| | **Chrono Shard** | pure temporal sequence; ensures timeline consistency | “Event 42 occurs after Scene 39” | | **Spatial Shard** | geospatial cluster — same city, venue, or room | “All shards set at **The Atrium**” | | **Perspective Shard** | same narrator or POV; tracks viewpoint continuity | “First-person Naomi perspective” | | **Thematic Shard** | clusters by motif, symbol, or recurring concept | “Blood / Mirror / Machine” cluster | | **Category Shard** | broad taxonomic grouping: media, logistics, lore | “Recording” / “Tour” / “Conflict” | | **Causal Shard** | encodes dependency graph — A causes B | “Announcement → Ticket Sale” | | **Intertext Shard** | cross-reference between canons or parallel texts | “Shattercore ↔ Repeater Canon” | | **Index Shard** | abstract ordering: alphabetical, numerical, color, etc. | “Color Index — Red Shards” | Each order shard adds a **layer of multidimensional order** — like metadata planes intersecting the same core text. --- ### 🔮 III.

FUNCTIONAL SHARDS — based on purpose or content These describe *what kind of information* the shard conveys. | Type | Domain | Function | |------|---------|-----------| | **Narrative Shard** | story content | characters, action, dialogue | | **Lore Shard** | world-building, mythos | cosmology, timelines, backstory | | **Meta Shard** | self-referential commentary | author notes, project logs | | **Technical Shard** | production details | recording gear, settings, logistics | | **Performance Shard** | live events, setlists | tours, festivals | | **Media Shard** | external reception | interviews, press, reviews | | **Analytic Shard** | derived interpretation | AI commentary, statistical patterns | | **System Shard** | programmatic or rule shards | pipeline scripts, data structures | | **Dream / Hypnagogic Shard** | altered-state or surreal narrative | liminal fragments | | **Null Shard** | intentionally empty shard (for pacing gaps) | placeholder of silence or loss | --- ### 🧩 IV.

META-HIERARCHY — “Shard of Shards” Each shard type can generate its own sub-shards: ``` Scene Shard ├─ Order Shards (Chrono, Spatial, Perspective) ├─ Function Shards (Narrative, Lore, Technical) └─ Structure Shards (Lines, Atoms) ``` Every shard has a **unique UUID** but also a **Shattercore Path**, e.g.: ``` BOOK.VISCERA / PART.I / CHAPTER.02A / SCENE.0047 / LINE.00032 ``` This gives absolute addressing, like frame coordinates in an editable timeline. --- ### 🧭 V.

Here’s how I’ll implement this in the next build pass (using the data you’ve already provided): --- ## ⚙️ SHATTERCORE TEMPORAL MODEL — “Every Shard Has Time” ### 1️⃣ Hierarchical Duration Cascade Each shard level inherits or aggregates duration from those below it. | Level | Formula | Example | |--------|----------|----------| | **Word (W)** | `len(word)/avg_chars_per_second` | e.g. “machine” → 0.4 s | | **Line (L)** | ∑ (word durations) | Sentence ≈ 6 s | | **Paragraph (P)** | ∑ (line durations) + transition | ≈ 25 s | | **Scene (S)** | ∑ (paragraphs) + pacing buffer | ≈ 3 min | | **Chapter (C)** | ∑ (scenes) | ≈ 20 min | | **Part (Pₐ)** | ∑ (chapters) | ≈ 1 h | | **Book (B)** | ∑ (parts) | total runtime | **Timing propagation:** If a scene’s lines are 3 s, 6 s, 4 s → the scene lasts 13 s.

Each shard will store: ```json "duration_sec": 42, "span_start_iso": "2045-07-22T22:11:00Z", "span_end_iso": "2045-07-22T22:11:42Z" ``` --- ### 2️⃣ Character-State Metadata at Every Level At **every shard scale**, the metadata block includes: ```json "characters": [ "name": "Naomi", "location": "Atrium", "activity": "listening to transmission", "interacting_with": ["Alex"], "motion_vector": "still", "speed_m_s": 0.0 , "name": "Alex", "location": "Atrium", "activity": "speaking", "interacting_with": ["Naomi"], "motion_vector": "toward exit", "speed_m_s": 1.2 ] ``` Rules: - Movement between scenes is paced realistically (distance ÷ time). - Activities persist until changed in a new shard. - Group motion (e.g., a tour bus) produces synchronized state updates across characters. --- ### 3️⃣ Scene Continuity Engine The engine will: 1.

u=m.group(2) if u.startswith("hour") or u in "hrs","hr" : return min(MAX_S, max(MIN_S, n*3600)) if u.startswith("min"): return min(MAX_S, max(MIN_S, n*60)) return min(MAX_S, max(MIN_S, n)) est = math.ceil(len(tokenize_words(t))/max(1e-6, WPS)) return min(MAX_S, max(MIN_S, est)) base["duration_sec"] = base["verbatim"].apply(duration_from_text) # Date/time normalization def to_iso_date(s): s = str(s).strip() if re.match(r'^ 4 - 2 - 2 $', s): return s m = re.match(r'^( 1,2 )/( 1,2 )/( 2,4 )$', s) if m: mm,dd,yy = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3)) if yy < 100: yy += 2000 if yy <= 69 else 1900 return f" yy:04d - mm:02d - dd:02d " return "" def parse_time(s): s=str(s).strip() m=re.search(r'( 1,2 ):( 2 )(?: *([AaPp][Mm]))?', s) if not m: return "" hh,mm,ap=int(m.group(1)),int(m.group(2)),m.group(3) if ap: ap=ap.lower() if ap=="pm" and hh!=12: hh+=12 if ap=="am" and hh==12: hh=0 return f" hh:02d : mm:02d " if "date" in base.columns: base["date_iso"] = base["date"].apply(to_iso_date) else: base["date_iso"] = "" if "time" in base.columns: base["time_iso"] = base["time"].apply(parse_time) else: base["time_iso"] = "" # Derive simple chronological key def origin_line(op): m=re.search(r':L( +) ', str(op)); `README.md` ```md # Shattercore Suite A monorepo for the Shattercore narrative engine: - `shattercore-fiction`: fractal story shards (structure, meta, segues), real-time chronology. - `shattercore-nonfiction`: investigative variant with provenance, contradictions, and evidentiary chains. - `shattercore-screenplay`: adapters to/from screenplay-friendly formats. - `shattercore-rtv`: hooks for a real-time visualizer. ## Quick start ```bash git clone  shattercore-suite cd shattercore-suite python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate pip install -e packages/shattercore-fiction -e packages/shattercore-nonfiction \ -e packages/shattercore-screenplay -e packages/shattercore-rtv ``` Validate a dataset (CSV/JSON): ```bash scf validate path/to/shard_core_index.csv scnf validate path/to/shard_core_index.csv ``` Pack all shards for distribution: ```bash scf pack path/to/folder out/shattercore_bundle.zip ``` See `packages/*/examples` for expected file names and schemas. ``` ## `packages/shattercore-fiction/pyproject.toml` ```toml [project] name = "shattercore-fiction" version = "2.0.0" description = "Shattercore engine for fiction" authors = [ name="You" ] readme = "README.md" requires-python = ">=3.10" dependencies = ["pandas>=2.1", "typer>=0.12", "rich>=13.7", "jsonschema>=4.22"] [project.scripts] scf = "shattercore_fiction.cli:app" [tool.setuptools.packages.find] where = ["src"] ``` ## `packages/shattercore-fiction/README.md` ```md # Shattercore (Fiction) ## CLI - `scf validate  [--schema ]` - `scf pack  ` - `scf convert --to parquet ` ## Expected files - `shard_core_index.csv` (all shards, any type) - `structure_scenes.csv` - `meta_shards.csv` - `order_shards.csv` - `segue_shards.csv` - `character_state_log.csv` See `schemas/*.schema.json` for columns and types. ``` ## `src/shattercore_fiction/__init__.py` ```python __all__ = ["validators"] ``` ## `src/shattercore_fiction/cli.py` ```python import typer, zipfile, json, sys from rich import print from .validators import validate_csv app = typer.Typer(add_completion=False) @app.command() def validate(file: str, schema: str = "auto"): ok, report = validate_csv(file, schema) if not ok: print("[red]Validation failed[/red]"); print(report); raise typer.Exit(code=1) print("[green]Validation passed[/green]"); print(report) @app.command() def pack(folder: str, out_zip: str): z = zipfile.ZipFile(out_zip, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) from pathlib import Path base = Path(folder) for p in base.rglob("*"): if p.is_file(): z.write(p, arcname=str(p.relative_to(base))) z.close() print(f"[green]Packed[/green] -> out_zip ") @app.command() def convert(file: str, to: str = "parquet"): import pandas as pd df = pd.read_csv(file) if to == "parquet": out = file.replace(".csv", ".parquet") df.to_parquet(out, index=False) print(f"[green]Converted[/green] -> out ") else: print("[red]Unsupported format[/red]"); raise typer.Exit(2) ``` ## `src/shattercore_fiction/validators.py` ```python import pandas as pd, json, jsonschema, pathlib SCHEMAS = "auto": "shard_core_index.schema.json", "index": "shard_core_index.schema.json", "meta": "meta_shards.schema.json", "scene": "structure_scenes.schema.json", "order": "order_shards.schema.json", "segue": "segue_shards.schema.json", "charlog": "character_state_log.schema.json", def _load_schema(name: str): here = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "schemas" with open(here / name, "r") as f: return json.load(f) def validate_csv(path: str, schema: str): df = pd.read_csv(path) name = SCHEMAS.get(schema, SCHEMAS["auto"]) schema_json = _load_schema(name) # Convert to records-of-strings to simplify validation recs = df.fillna("").astype(str).to_dict(orient="records") try: jsonschema.validate( "rows": recs , schema_json) return True, "rows": len(recs), "schema": name except jsonschema.ValidationError as e: return False, "schema": name, "error": str(e) ``` ## Example schema (fiction) — `schemas/shard_core_index.schema.json` ```json "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema", "title":"Shattercore Index (Fiction)", "type":"object", "properties": "rows": "type":"array", "items": "type":"object", "properties": "id": "type":"string" , "type": "enum":["base_shard","structure","meta","segue"] , "scale": "type":"string" , "title": "type":"string" , "start_iso": "type":"string" , "end_iso": "type":"string" , "duration_sec": "type":["integer","string"] , "location": "type":"string" , "characters": "type":"string" , "parent_id": "type":"string" , "children_ids": "type":"string" , "required":["id","type","start_iso","end_iso","duration_sec"] , "required":["rows"] ``` (You’ll also have JSON schemas for `meta_shards`, `structure_scenes`, `order_shards`, `segue_shards`, and `character_state_log` in the same folder, matching the columns we’ve been using.) --- # 3) Shattercore-NF (Nonfiction / Investigatory) ## `packages/shattercore-nonfiction/pyproject.toml` ```toml [project] name = "shattercore-nonfiction" version = "1.0.0" description = "Shattercore engine for nonfiction/investigations" authors = [ name="You" ] readme = "README.md" requires-python = ">=3.10" dependencies = ["pandas>=2.1", "typer>=0.12", "rich>=13.7", "jsonschema>=4.22"] [project.scripts] scnf = "shattercore_nf.cli:app" [tool.setuptools.packages.find] where = ["src"] ``` ## `src/shattercore_nf/cli.py` ```python import typer, json from rich import print from .validators import validate_csv app = typer.Typer(add_completion=False) @app.command() def validate(file: str, schema: str = "index"): ok, report = validate_csv(file, schema) if not ok: print("[red]Validation failed[/red]"); print(report); raise typer.Exit(1) print("[green]Validation passed[/green]"); print(report) ``` ## `src/shattercore_nf/validators.py` ```python import pandas as pd, json, jsonschema, pathlib SCHEMAS = "index": "shard_core_index.schema.json", "evidence": "evidence_shards.schema.json", "agent": "agent_timeline.schema.json", "contradiction": "contradiction_map.schema.json", "order": "order_shards.schema.json", "segue": "segue_shards.schema.json" def _load_schema(name): here = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "schemas" with open(here / name, "r") as f: return json.load(f) def validate_csv(path, which): df = pd.read_csv(path) recs = df.fillna("").astype(str).to_dict(orient="records") schema = _load_schema(SCHEMAS[which]) try: jsonschema.validate( "rows": recs , schema) return True, "rows": len(recs), "schema": which except jsonschema.ValidationError as e: return False, "schema": which, "error": str(e) ``` ## Example schema (nonfiction) — `schemas/evidence_shards.schema.json` ```json "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema", "title":"Evidence Shards", "type":"object", "properties": "rows": "type":"array", "items": "type":"object", "properties": "evidence_id": "type":"string" , "source_title": "type":"string" , "source_type": "enum":["doc","transcript","interview","photo","video","database","web"] , "source_uri": "type":"string" , "captured_at": "type":"string" , "claim_text": "type":"string" , "quoted_text": "type":"string" , "attribution": "type":"string" , "reliability_score": "type":["number","string"] , "bias_tags": "type":"string" , "link_to_shard": "type":"string" , "required":["evidence_id","source_type","captured_at","link_to_shard"] , "required":["rows"] ``` ## Example schema — `schemas/contradiction_map.schema.json` ```json "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema", "title":"Contradiction Map", "type":"object", "properties": "rows": "type":"array", "items": "type":"object", "properties": "contradiction_id": "type":"string" , "claim_a_id": "type":"string" , "claim_b_id": "type":"string" , "dimension": "enum":["time","location","identity","quantity","causality"] , "severity": "type":["number","string"] , "status": "enum":["open","resolved","flagged"] , "resolution_note": "type":"string" , "required":["contradiction_id","claim_a_id","claim_b_id","dimension","status"] , "required":["rows"] ``` --- # 4) Extras ## Shattercore Canon Processor (you run locally) Save this script as `process_canon.py` inside your suite and execute it: ```python """ Reads all VISCERA canon text files, parses shards, and outputs a single canon_master.csv with every possible linkage and metadata field. """ import re, csv, uuid, datetime, hashlib from pathlib import Path import pandas as pd UTC = datetime.datetime.utcnow().replace(microsecond=0).isoformat() + "Z" SRC = [ Path("VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt"), Path("SHATTERCORE_ALL.txt"), ] out = Path("canon_master.csv") rows = [] for src in SRC: if not src.exists(): continue text = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") blocks = re.split(r" * ", text) for i, blk in enumerate(blocks): blk = blk.strip() if not blk: continue shard_id = f"canon_ src.stem _ i:05d " # Basic analytics word_count = len(re.findall(r" +", blk)) char_count = len(blk) md5 = hashlib.md5(blk.encode()).hexdigest() rows.append( "shard_id": shard_id, "source_file": src.name, "index": i, "word_count": word_count, "char_count": char_count, "md5": md5, "version_semver": "2.0.0", "created_at": UTC, "last_modified": UTC, "shattercore_build_id": uuid.uuid4().hex, "text": blk, ) df = pd.DataFrame(rows) df.to_csv(out, index=False) print(f"✅ Wrote len(df) shards to out ") ``` Running this creates `canon_master.csv`, a table of **every line of your canon**, uniquely ID’d and fingerprinted. --- ### ⚙️ 2.

Automatic linking & metadata enrichment After generating `canon_master.csv`, run: ```python python shattercore-suite/packages/shattercore-fiction/src/shattercore_fiction/cli.py enrich canon_master.csv ``` This call (once you implement or extend `enrich`) can attach: | Field | Description | |--------|--------------| | `theme_tags` | Extracted motifs & imagery clusters | | `emotion_vector` | Sentiment gradient (–1.0 → 1.0) | | `style_shard` | fast-paced, introspective, ironic, etc. | | `motif_links` | recurrent phrase hashes | | `segue_targets` | adjacency relationships | | `context_window` | 3 preceding / following shard IDs | | `chronology_iso` | estimated timeline placement | --- ### 🧠 3.

""" import re, csv, uuid, datetime, hashlib from pathlib import Path import pandas as pd UTC = datetime.datetime.utcnow().replace(microsecond=0).isoformat() + "Z" sources = [ Path("VISCERA_all_text_consolidated.txt"), Path("SHATTERCORE_ALL.txt"), Path("VISCERA_Canon_Master.xlsx"), # if converted to CSV sheets ] rows = [] for src in sources: if not src.exists(): continue if src.suffix in [".txt"]: text = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") blocks = re.split(r" * ", text) for i, blk in enumerate(blocks): blk = blk.strip() if not blk: continue sid = f" src.stem _ i:05d " md5 = hashlib.md5(blk.encode()).hexdigest() rows.append( "shard_id": sid, "source": src.name, "index": i, "md5": md5, "word_count": len(blk.split()), "char_count": len(blk), "version_semver": "2.0.0", "created_at": UTC, "last_modified": UTC, "shattercore_build_id": uuid.uuid4().hex, "text": blk ) elif src.suffix in [".xlsx"]: xl = pd.read_excel(src, sheet_name=None) for name, df in xl.items(): for i, row in df.iterrows(): text = " ".join(str(x) for x in row if isinstance(x, str)) if not text.strip(): continue sid = f" name _ i:05d " md5 = hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest() rows.append( "shard_id": sid, "source": f" src.name : name ", "index": i, "md5": md5, "word_count": len(text.split()), "char_count": len(text), "version_semver": "2.0.0", "created_at": UTC, "last_modified": UTC, "shattercore_build_id": uuid.uuid4().hex, "text": text ) df = pd.DataFrame(rows) # Urban legends have a distinctive rhythm: they start in a normal world, slowly introduce an element of the bizarre, and build to a shocking or ironic climax that often carries a cautionary message.

So, show the mundane funeral setting and bureaucratic process authentically – then introduce the one extraordinary twist (the frantic exhumation) as if it were a truth-is-stranger-than-fiction occurrence. > To emulate urban legend tone, consider including the following elements in Alma’s telling (even if subtly): > Believability anchored by specifics: She might mention the exact Virginia cemetery, the date, or a (fictional) newspaper clipping – little touches that say “this really happened.” A legend often has a *“kernel of truth”*. > Gradual build-up of suspense: Perhaps at first everything seems normal (the family buries the mother after a supposed aneurysm), then odd signs (maybe Alma had a nagging feeling, or a witness heard something) escalate to the shocking discovery.

Generate full ASCII (0x20–0x7E) character shard registry as JSONL and CSV import json, pandas as pd, os, unicodedata entries = [] for cp in range(0x20, 0x7F): # inclusive of 0x7E ch = chr(cp) name = unicodedata.name(ch, f"UNKNOWN U+ cp:04X ") category = unicodedata.category(ch) # e.g., 'Lu', 'Nd', 'Po' entry = "id": f"shard:char:U+ cp:04X ", "type": "CHAR", "attrs": "char": ch, "name": name, "codepoint_hex": f"U+ cp:04X ", "codepoint_dec": cp, "unicode_category": category, "ascii": True, "aliases": [], "bytes_utf8": [cp], # ASCII == UTF-8 single byte "norm": "NFC": ch, "NFD": ch, "NFKC": ch, "NFKD": ch , "status": "asserted", "confidence": 1.0, "prov": ["charset:ASCII"], "version": 1 # add case and whitespace flags if 'L' in category: entry["attrs"]["case"] = "upper" if ch.isupper() else "lower" if ch.islower() else "na" if ch in [' ', ' ', ' ', ' ', ' b', ' c']: entry["attrs"]["is_whitespace"] = True entries.append(entry) # Helper: safe read text def read_text(path, limit=None): try: with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f: data = f.read() if limit and len(data) > limit: return data[:limit] + " [...truncated...]" return data except Exception as e: return f"[[ERROR READING path : e ]]" # Collect files text_exts = ".txt",".md",".log",".py",".js",".ts",".html",".css" json_exts = ".json",".jsonl",".ndjson" csv_exts = ".csv",".tsv" yaml_exts = ".yaml",".yml" pdf_exts = ".pdf" inventory = [] for root, dirs, files in os.walk(EXTRACT_DIR): for fname in files: fpath = os.path.join(root, fname) rel = os.path.relpath(fpath, EXTRACT_DIR) ext = pathlib.Path(fpath).suffix.lower() size = os.path.getsize(fpath) inventory.append( "rel": rel, "ext": ext, "size": size, "path": fpath ) # Sort by type then name inventory.sort(key=lambda r: (r["ext"], r["rel"])) # Shard containers shards = "legal": [], "communications": [], "technical": [], "data_tables": [], "financial": [], "people_entities": [], "timeline": [], "misc": [] def hash_text(s): return hashlib.sha1(s.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()[:10] # Simple heuristics for shard routing LEGAL_PAT = re.compile(r" (complaint|subpoena|warrant|lawsuit|statute|code §| \.S\.C\. |regulation|contract|terms of service|privacy policy|nda|non[- ]?disclosure|affidavit|deposition|motion|court|plaintiff|defendant) ", re.I) COMMS_PAT = re.compile(r" (from|to|subject|cc|bcc):|@[ .-]+| :| :", re.I) TECH_PAT = re.compile(r" (import |def |class |function|var |let |const |SELECT |INSERT |UPDATE |DELETE |docker|kubernetes|api|endpoint|schema|json) ", re.I) FIN_PAT = re.compile(r" (invoice|amount|total|balance|account|transaction|bank|wire|ACH|routing|IBAN|paid|unpaid) ", re.I) DATE_PAT = re.compile(r" (20 2 [-/\.](0?[1-9]|1[0-2])[-/\.](0?[1-9]|[12] |3[01])| (Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec) \.? + 1,2 , +20 2 )", re.I) PERSON_PAT = re.compile(r" ([A-Z][a-z]+(?: +[A-Z][a-z]+) 1,2 ) ") #
